## Corpus Check
- 1055 files · ~1,995,955 words
- Verdict: corpus is large enough that graph structure adds value.

## Summary
- 18769 nodes · 36309 edges · 1115 communities (915 shown, 200 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 95% EXTRACTED · 5% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 1839 edges (avg confidence: 0.89)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `e6fec287`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
